```python
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog? maybe not.

def next_pow2(x): return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()

def fft_conv(a):
    n=len(a); N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)
    return c[:2*n-1]

def fft_corr_w_a(w,a):
    n=len(a); N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conj(fa),N)
    return r[:n]
```

Project onto simplex sum=t:
Standard: project y onto {x≥0, sum x=t}. We need efficient. Find threshold τ such that sum(max(y-τ,0))=t. Use sorting O(n log n). n 4806 okay; many calls maybe use flat algorithm? Sorting 4806 ~ fast. Implement:
```
def project_simplex(y, target):
    y=np.asarray(y)
    if y.sum() == target: return max...
    u=np.sort(y)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,len(u)+1)) > (css[-1]-target))[0][-1]
    theta=(css[rho]-target)/(rho+1.0)
    return np.maximum(y-theta,0)
```
Need target = n. If y large. This projects in l2. Could be slow if called every backtracking. But okay. Could implement faster using `scipy.optimize?` no. Maybe use `nelder?` no.

But L-BFGS softmax doesn't need projection. We could skip projection entirely by using softmax. However projection gradient can handle zeros.

```
def lse_val(u):

    z=(c-M)/CUR_TAU
    # stable: max z=0
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    return M + CUR_TAU*np.log(s)
```

```
    z=(c-M)/tau; w=exp(z)/sum
```

Multi-scale procedure with LP:
```
def lp_iterate(f, n, max_time, max_iters):
    best_f=f; best_score=exact_score(f)
    t=0.2? 
    for iter:
       M=max conv(f)
       solve LP min z for f
       if fail break
       g=res.x[:n]; normalize mean n
       for mix in [0.5,0.3,0.15,0.075,...]:
          h=(1-mix)*f+m*g; normalize
          sc=exact_score(h)
          if sc<best: update; keep g? break
       if no improvement many or same g break
       # anneal t lower
    return best
```

Let's implement exact active gradient without FFT for polish:
```
def active_gradient(b, K):
    c=fft_conv(b)  # or if exact score we have c
    M=c.max()
    order=np.argsort(c)[-K:]
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for d in order:
        lo=max(0,d-n+1); hi=min(n-1,d)
        # derivative of c_d: 2 * b[d-i] for i in lo..hi
        # d-i ranges d-hi .. d-lo
        g[lo:hi+1] += 2.0 * b[d-hi:d-lo+1][::-1]
    g /= K
    return g
```
This O(K*segment) C slicing. K=50 average segment ~n/2? For d around center, slice length n; 50*4806=240k additions, 1ms. Good.

```
def optimize_pg(b, time_limit, tau_rels=(0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01), alpha=0.05, exact_top=0):
   b=mean1_copy(b); n=len(b)
   best_b=b; best_score=exact_score_b(b)
   alpha=alpha
   for tau_rel in tau_rels:
      M=...
      tau=tau_rel*M
      stalls=0
      while time remaining and stalls<20:
         c=fft_conv(b); M=c.max(); score=2*M/n
         if score<best: update
         if tau>0:
            w=softmax_c(c,tau)
            gb=2*fft_corr_w_a(w,b)
         else:
            gb=exact_top_gradient(b,c,top=... )
         normg2=dot(gb,gb)
         if normg2<1e-18: break
         # line search
         f0 = M + tau*logsumexp if tau>0 else M  # smooth f
         step=b-alpha*gb
         y=project_simplex(step,n)
         f1=... 
         best_alpha=alpha
         accepted=False
         for _ in range(10):
             y=project_simplex(b-alpha*gb,n)
             if y finite:
                f1=smooth_value(y,tau)
                # use Armijo c1=1e-4 * alpha*normg2? For smooth
                if f1 < f0 - 1e-4*alpha*normg2:
                    accepted=True; break
             alpha*=0.5
         if not accepted:
             # Try just accept if exact improves
             y=project_simplex(b-alpha*gb,n); sc=exact_score(y); if sc<best: b=y; score update; else break/perturb
         else:
             b=y
             # Barzilai update alpha maybe
             alpha = min(2.0, alpha*1.05) if improvement good else alpha*0.8
             stalls=0
         if not improvement:
             stalls+=1; alpha*=0.7
      # after tau stage, exact polish top-K
   return best_b,best_score
```

```
def polish_exact(b, time_limit, top_vals=(50,20,10,5,3,2,1), alpha=0.02):
   b=mean1_copy; best_score=...
   for top in top_vals:
       alpha=0.05? 
       no_improve=0
       while time and no_improve<100:
          c=fft_conv(b); score=...
          if score<best: update; no_improve=0
          # weights: top K with softmax if close? If K=1 exact
          order=np.argsort(c)[-top:]
          # weight by positive part c - (M-0.02M)? maybe.
          w=np.zeros(2n-1); w[order]=1.0/top
          gb=weighted_active_grad(b,w)
          # try a few alphas: alpha, alpha/2, alpha*2? Project
          best_step=None
          for factor in [1,0.5,0.25,2.0]:
             a=alpha*factor
             y=project_simplex(b-a*gb,n)
             sc=exact_score_b(y)
             if sc < score - 1e-12:
                 best_step=y; break
          if best_step is None:
             no_improve+=1
             alpha*=0.7
             if no_improve>50: break
             # occasional random perturb
          else:
             b=best_step; no_improve=0; alpha*=1.1 (cap)
   return best
```

`project_simplex`: Need efficient and robust. Input y maybe large; target positive. The formula with sorted descending:
```
def project_simplex(y, target):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    s = y.sum()
    if np.isclose(s,target,rtol=1e-12,atol=1e-12):
        z=np.maximum(y,0)
        zsum=z.sum()
        if zsum>0: return z*target/zsum
        return np.full_like(y,target/len(y))
    u=np.sort(y)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    lens=np.arange(1,len(u)+1)
    cond = u*lens > (css[-1]-target)
    if not np.any(cond):
       theta=(css[-1]-target)/len(u)
       return np.maximum(y-theta,0)
    rho=int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta=(css[rho]-target)/(rho+1.0)
    z=np.maximum(y-theta,0)
    zsum=z.sum()
    if zsum>0:
       return z*(target/zsum)
    return np.full_like(y,target/len(y))
```
The rescale after projection may violate exact target if sum not; fine.

```
def active_gradient_from_c(b, c, K=20, tau_frac=None):
    n=len(b); m=len(c)
    M=c.max()
    idx=np.argpartition(c, max(0,m-K))[-K:] maybe.
    if tau_frac is not None and tau_frac>0:
       # use softmax over all? For weighted active, could compute exp((c-M)/(tau_frac*M)) and drop tiny.
       if tau_frac*M > 0:
          wc = np.exp((c-M)/(tau_frac*M)); threshold=wc.max()*1e-8; idx=np.nonzero(wc>threshold)[0]
          if len(idx)==0: idx=np.argmax(c)
          w=wc[idx]/wc[idx].sum()
       else: uniform
    else: uniform on top K
    g=zeros
    for d,wt in zip(idx,w):
       lo=max(0,d-n+1); hi=min(n-1,d)
       if lo<=hi:
          g[lo:hi+1] += wt * 2.0 * b[hi? Need slice]
    return g
```
Slice: For d, i from lo to hi. j=d-i. When i=lo, j=d-lo (max); i=hi, j=d-hi (min). So b slice from d-lo down to d-hi inclusive: `b[d-lo:d-hi-1:-1]`? In Python, if slice start > stop with step -1 includes start, stops before stop. Need if d-hi could be 0. Example n=3,d=2, lo=0,hi=2, partners j=2,1,0. `b[2:-1:-1]` gives b[2],b[1] (stops at -1 exclusive? Actually -1 is n-1? bad). Better use positive/zero stop: `b[d-lo:d-hi-1:-1]`; if d-hi=0, `b[2:-1:-1]` still stops before -1 (which is index n-1?), wrong. Use reversed of slice: `b[min(n, d-hi): d-lo+1][::-1]`? `b[d-hi:d-lo+1][::-1]`. Example d=2, d-hi=0,d-lo=2 -> b[0:3][::-1]=b2,b1,b0. Good. For d=0,n=1 lo=hi=0 -> b[0:1][::-1]. Good. Line search for smooth PG:
```
def smooth_pg_search(b, tau, alpha, max_trials=8):
    c=fft_conv(b); f0=smooth_value_from_c(c,tau)
    M=c.max()
    z=np.exp((c-M)/tau); w=z/z.sum()
    gb=2*fft_corr(w,b)
    normg2=dot(gb,gb)
    if not finite or normg2==0: return b,f0,gb,0
    best_alpha=None; best_y=None; best_f=inf
    a=alpha
    for _ in range(max_trials):
       y=project_simplex(b - a*gb, n)
       if finite:
          cy=fft_conv(y); fy=smooth_value_from_c(cy,tau)
          if fy < best_f: best_f=fy; best_y=y; best_alpha=a
          if fy < f0 - 1e-6*a*normg2:
              return y, fy, gb, a, True
       a*=0.5
       if a<1e-12: break
    return best_y,best_f,gb,best_alpha, False
```

Upscale function:
```
def upscale(b_old, n_new):
   m=len(b_old)
   if m==n_new: return mean1_copy(b_old)
   old_x = np.linspace(0,1,m)
   new_x = np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
   # use np.interp
   v=np.interp(new_x, old_x, b_old, left=b_old[0], right=b_old[-1])
   # Optionally PCHIP if n_old>=4
   v2 = None
   try:
      from scipy.interpolate import PchipInterpolator
      pchip=PchipInterpolator(old_x,b_old)
      v2=np.maximum(pchip(new_x),0)
   except: pass
   candidates=[v, v2, np.interp(new_x, old_x, b_old, left=0,right=0)?]
   return mean1_copy(v)
```

```
def mean1_copy(arr, target_mean=None):
   a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64).copy()
   a=~finite? set 0; clip 0
   if target_mean is None: target_mean=float(len(a)); else target_mean
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=1e-15: a.fill(target_mean/len(a))
   else: a *= target_mean/s
   return a
```

```
def exact_polish(b, time_limit):
   n=len(b); b=mean1_copy(best)
   best_score=exact_score_b(b); best_b=b
   K_seq = [200,100,50,30,20,10,5,3,2,1]
   alpha=0.05
   for K in K_seq:
      no=0; alpha=0.05
      while time left and no<100:
         c=fft_conv(b); M=c.max(); score=2*M/n (or exact)
         if score<best: update; no=0
         # weights: if K>1, use top K with weight proportional to exp((c-M)/(0.01*M)) maybe
         if K == len(c): uniform? no
         idx = np.argpartition(c, max(0, len(c)-K))[-K:] if K<len(c) else np.arange(len(c))
         # For K large, use softmax weights over top:
         vals=c[idx]
         w=np.exp((vals-vals.max())/(max(1e-12,0.02*vals.max())))
         w/=w.sum()
         g=weighted_active_grad(b, idx, w)
         # Try alphas: alpha, alpha*0.5, alpha*2
         best_y=None; best_sc=score
         for factor in [1.0,0.5,0.25,2.0]:
             a=alpha*factor
             if a<=0: continue
             y=project_simplex(b - a*g, n)
             if y finite:
                sc=exact_score_b(y)
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc=sc; best_y=y
         if best_y is not None:
             b=best_y; no=0; alpha=min(0.5, alpha*1.05)
         else:
             no+=1; alpha*=0.8
             if no>10:
                 # add tiny random perturb
                 b = mean1_copy(b + rng.normal(0, 1e-4, n))
                 no=0? maybe
      # after each K if time...
   return best_b,best_score
```

Weighted active gradient with idx/w:
```
def weighted_active_grad(b, idx, w):
   n=len(b); g=np.zeros(n)
   for d,wt in zip(idx,w):
      if wt <= 1e-15: continue
      lo=max(0,int(d)-n+1); hi=min(n-1,int(d))
      if lo<=hi:
         g[lo:hi+1] += wt * 2.0 * b[d-hi:d-lo+1][::-1]
   return g
```
This loops; for K=200, 200 slices length up to n; 1M operations, okay. But called many steps; maybe heavy. Use FFT for K large? Can choose if K>50 use FFT weights. We can compute weights vector length 2n-1 and use `fft_corr`. For K small direct. Implement both:
```
def active_gradient_by_weights(b, c, K, tau_frac=0.02):
   m=len(c)
   if K>100:
      M=c.max(); tau=tau_frac*M
      w_all=np.exp((c-M)/tau)
      if w_all.sum()<=0: w_all=0; w_all[np.argmax(c)]=1
      w_all/=w_all.sum()
      # zero out below K? Optional: if K small, set non-top zero
      if K < m:
         threshold=np.partition(w_all, m-K)[m-K]
         w_all[w_all < threshold*1e-6]=0; w_all/=w_all.sum()
      h=fft_corr(w_all,b); return 2*h
   else direct top K
```
But `fft_corr` needs N for corr; okay. For K large efficient.

Low-scale LP optimization function:
```
def optimize_small_lp(b, time_limit, max_iters=100):
   b=mean1_copy(b)
   best=...
   it=0
   while time left and it<max_iters:
      g=lp_direction(b)
      if g is None: break
      # line search mix values
      best_mix=None
      for mix in [0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125]:
         h=mean1_copy((1-mix)*b + mix*g)
         sc=exact_score_b(h)
         if sc<best_score: best_score=sc; best_b=h; best_mix=mix
      if best_mix is None:
         # no improvement, reduce mix? break or add random
         it++ ; if many break; maybe try smaller mix 0.01
      else:
         b=best_b
      it++
   return best_b,best_score
```

Helpers:
```
def next_pow2(ll):
    if ll <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(ll)-1).bit_length()

def get_fft_size(n):
    if n in _FFT_CACHE: return _FFT_CACHE[n]
    N = next_pow2(max(1, 3*n-2))
    _FFT_CACHE[n]=N
    return N
```
For n maybe 0? not.

`fft_conv`:
```
def fft_conv(a):
    n=len(a)
    N=get_fft_size(n)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    # clip tiny negative
    c[c<0] = 0.0
    return c
```
For real, `fa*fa` okay (not conj; for autoconv of same real, square). If a has tiny imaginary? no.

`fft_corr`:
```
def fft_corr(w,a):
    n=len(a)
    N=get_fft_size(n)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), N)
    r=r[:n]
    return r
```
Need ensure w length 2n-1; if N >= len(w)+len(a) ✓.

`softmax`:
```
def softmax(x):
    x=np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    mx=x.max()
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return np.full(len(x), 1.0/len(x))
    e=np.exp(x-mx)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return uniform
    return e/s
```

`mean1_copy`:
```
def mean1_copy(arr, target=None, upper=1000.0):
   if arr is None: return None
   a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64).copy()
   a[~np.isfinite(a)]=0.0
   a[a<0]=0.0
   if target is None: target=float(len(a))
   s=a.sum()
   if s <= 1e-15:
       a.fill(target/len(a))
   else:
       a *= target/s
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,upper)
   # redistribute if clipping changed sum?
   for _ in range(5):
       s=a.sum()
       if s > 0 and abs(s-target) <= 1e-8*max(1,target): break
       # if max at upper after scaling, add deficit to below
       mask = a < upper - 1e-9
       if mask.sum()==0:
           a.fill(target/len(a)); break
       deficit=target-s
       if deficit>0:
           add=min(deficit/mask.sum(), upper - a[mask].min() if mask.any() else 0.0)
           a[mask]+=add
       else:
           # subtract uniformly
           red=min(-deficit/mask.sum(), a[mask].min())
           a[mask]-=red
   a[a<0]=0
   return a
```
This is overkill; maybe simpler.

`exact_score_b(b)`:
```
def exact_score_b(b):
   b=np.asarray(b)
   if len(b)==0: return inf
   s=b.sum()
   if s <= 1e-12: return inf
   c=fft_conv(b)
   mc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
   if mc<0: mc=0
   return float(2.0*len(b)*mc/(s*s))
```

`smooth_value_from_c(c, tau)`:
```
def smooth_value_from_c(c, tau):
   M=float(c.max())
   if tau <= 1e-15: return M
   z=(c-M)/tau
   e=np.exp(z)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: return M
   return float(M + tau*np.log(s))
```

`weights_from_c(c, tau)`: similar, return w.

`smooth_grad_b(b, tau)`:
```
def smooth_grad_b(b, tau):
   c=fft_conv(b)
   M=c.max()
   if tau <=1e-15:
       d=int(np.argmax(c)); w=np.zeros(len(c)); w[d]=1; 
   else:
       e=np.exp((c-M)/tau); w=e/e.sum()
   h=fft_corr(w,b)
   return 2.0*h, c
```
But FFT corr with w length m works. If tau small and w one, okay. For K direct maybe faster.

LSE funcs:
```
def lse_objective(u):
    n=_LSE_N
    b=n*softmax(u)
    c=fft_conv(b)
    M=float(c.max())
    tau=_LSE_TAU
    if tau <=1e-15:
       return float(2.0*M/n)  # score? objective scale by 2/n? We can use score to align.
    e=np.exp((c-M)/tau); s=e.sum()
    val = M + tau*np.log(s)
    # Use score-like 2*val/n for L-BFGS? Constant factor no matter. Use val.
    return float(2.0*val/n)
```
If objective is score, gradient needs scale 2/n. We can either minimize val and gradient val; factor constant no effect. Use val for simplicity. But near exact, L-BFGS not sensitive. Return val.
```
def lse_gradient(u):
    n=_LSE_N
    b=n*softmax(u)
    c=fft_conv(b)
    M=c.max(); tau=_LSE_TAU
    if tau<=1e-15:
        w=zeros; w[argmax]=1
    else: e...
    h=fft_corr(w,b)
    gb=2*h
    g=b*(gb - np.dot(b,gb)/n)
    if not finite: return zeros
    return g
```
If we return val not score, okay.

```
def run_pg(b, time_limit, tau_rels=(0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005), alpha=0.05):
    b=mean1_copy(b); n=len(b); best_b=b; best_score=...

    for tau_rel in tau_rels:
       if rem<10 break
       M=fft_conv(b).max(); tau=tau_rel*M
       stalls=0
       while rem>5 and stalls<40:
          c=fft_conv(b); f0=smooth_value_from_c(c,tau)
          M=c.max()
          e=np.exp((c-M)/tau); w=e/e.sum()
          h=fft_corr(w,b); gb=2*h
          ng2=float(np.dot(gb,gb))
          if not finite or ng2<1e-18: break
          accepted=False; best_y=None; best_f=f0; best_a=alpha
          a=alpha
          for _ in range(5):

             alpha=min(0.5, best_a*1.1)

          else:

    # final exact small polish maybe
```

Now full candidate optimizer:
```
def optimize_candidate(b, time_limit, use_lbfgs=True, use_pg=True, use_exact=True):
    b=mean1_copy(b)
    best_b=b; best_score=exact_score_b(b)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    if use_lbfgs and time_limit>60:
       t_lbfgs = min(time_limit-60, max(30, 0.5*time_limit))
       b1,s1=run_lbfgs(best_b,t_lbfgs)
       update
    if use_pg and time_left>30:
       t_pg=min(time_left-30, max(10,0.25*time_limit))
       b2,s2=run_pg(best_b,t_pg)
       update
    if use_exact and time_left>10:
       b3,s3=exact_polish(best_b,time_left)
       update
    return best_b,best_score
```

Low-scale LP functions:
`lp_direction(f)`:
```
def lp_direction(f):
    if linprog is None: return None
    n=len(f)
    if n>800: return None
    f_pos=mean1_copy(f)
    # ensure lower bound tiny to avoid unbounded? if min<1e-8, f_pos = f_pos + 1e-7*mean; renormalize
    f_pos = np.maximum(f_pos, 1e-7)
    f_pos = f_pos/f_pos.sum()*n
    A=np.zeros((2*n-1, n+1), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
       A[j:j+n, j] = f_pos  # length n full assignment? col slice length n yes
    A[:, n] = -1.0
    cobj=np.zeros(n+1); cobj[-1]=1
    Aeq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1
    beq=np.array([float(n)])
    bounds=[(0,None)]*n + [(0,None)]
    res=linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.zeros(2*n-1), A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=beq, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':True})
    if res.status !=0: return None
    g=res.x[:n]
    g=np.maximum(g,0)
    s=g.sum()
    if s <=1e-9: return None
    g*=n/s
    # cap
    return mean1_copy(g)
```
Will `A[j:j+n,j]=f_pos` assign f_pos length n to slice length n, good. For n=800, loop 800.

```
def optimize_small_lp(b, time_limit, max_iters=200):
    b=mean1_copy(b); n=len(b); best=b; best_score=...
    t0=...
    it=0; no_improve=0
    mixes=[0.5,0.3,0.15,0.075,0.0375,0.01875]
    while time left and it<max_iters:
       g=lp_direction(b)
       if g is None: break
       improved=False
       for mix in mixes:
          h=mean1_copy((1-mix)*b + mix*g)
          sc=exact_score_b(h)
          if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
             best_score=sc; best=h; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
          # try tiny mix 0.005
          h=mean1_copy((1-0.005)*b + 0.005*g); sc=...
       if not improved:
          no_improve+=1; if no_improve>10: break
          # perturb b slightly? maybe
          b=mean1_copy(best + 1e-3*RNG.normal(size=n))
       else:
          b=best; it++; no_improve=0
    return best,best_score
```

```
def make_lowscale_seeds(parent, N, time_limit):
    seeds=[]
    if parent is None: return seeds
    # n_small list
    for ns in [128,256]:
       if time left < 30: break
       inits=[]
       inits.append(downsample(parent, ns))
       inits.append(np.ones(ns))
       # cosine best
       for k in [2,4,8]: inits.append(mean1_copy(1+0.5*np.cos(2πk i/ns)))
       # random
       inits.append(mean1_copy(1+0.3*RNG.normal(ns)))
       # optimize top? just run each with time small
       t_each=max(10, time_limit/(len(inits))*0.7)

           b,s=optimize_small_lp(init, t_each)
           seeds.append(b)
       if seeds: seeds sort by small exact? Not comparable but okay.
    return seeds
```

Upscale:
```
def upscale_sequence(b_old, n_new):
   m=len(b_old)
   if m==n_new: return mean1_copy(b_old)
   old_pos=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
   new_pos=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
   cands=[]
   c1=np.interp(new_pos, old_pos, b_old, left=b_old[0], right=b_old[-1])
   c2=np.interp(new_pos, old_pos, b_old, left=0, right=0)
   if PchipInterpolator is not None and m>=4:
       try:
          pp=PchipInterpolator(old_pos, b_old)
          c3=np.maximum(pp(new_pos),0)
          cands.append(c3)
       except: pass
   # repeat block if m divides n? 
   if n_new % m ==0:
       c4=np.tile(b_old, n_new//m)
       cands.append(c4)
   # choose first or average? return linear
   best=None; best_sc=inf
   for c in cands:
       c=mean1_copy(c)
       sc=exact_score_b(c)
       if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best=c
   return best
```
PCHIP on non-monotone can go negative; clip.

```
   # sparse
   ...
```

Random patterns functions:
```
def random_smooth_init(n, rng, order=8):
   t=np.arange(n)
   base=np.ones(n)
   for k in range(1,order+1):
      amp=0.1/range? 
      phase=rng.random()*2π
      base += amp*np.cos(2πk t/n + phase)
   return mean1_copy(np.maximum(base,0.01))
```
Sparse:
```
def sparse_init(n, rng, C, height=5, base=0.2):
   x=np.full(n, base)
   pos=np.sort(rng.choice(n, size=C, replace=False))
   x[pos]=rng.uniform(1, height)
   return mean1_copy(x)
```
Cap.

```
   # final sanitize, maybe evaluate_sequence

   # try evaluate_sequence to ensure finite; if not, use best from local? 
   print final score
   return [float(v) for v in final]
```

Detailed function definitions:

```
def downsample_sequence(b_old, n_new):
    if b_old is None: return np.ones(n_new)
    old=np.asarray(b_old,float)
    m=len(old)
    if m==n_new: return mean1_copy(old,n_new)
    old_pos=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    new_pos=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
    out=np.interp(new_pos, old_pos, old, left=old[0], right=old[-1])
    return mean1_copy(out,n_new)
```

`make_direct_patterns(n, best_b)`:
```
def make_direct_patterns(n, best_b):
    pats=[]
    if best_b is not None:
       pats.append(mean1_copy(best_b,n))
    pats.append(np.ones(n))
    t=np.arange(n)
    for c in [0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0]:
       x=np.exp(-c*t/max(1,n-1)); pats.append(mean1_copy(x,n)); pats.append(mean1_copy(x[::-1],n))
    for k in [1,2,3,4,6,8]:
       for A in [0.3,0.6,0.9]:
          x=1.0 + A*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*t/n)
          pats.append(mean1_copy(np.maximum(x,0.01),n))
          x=1.0 + A*np.sin(2*np.pi*k*t/n)
          pats.append(mean1_copy(np.maximum(x,0.01),n))
    # two blocks
    for p in [0.1,0.15,0.2,0.3,0.4]:
       for r in [0.4,0.7,1.5,3.0]:
          x=np.ones(n); x[:max(1,int(p*n))]=r; pats.append(mean1_copy(x,n))
          x=np.ones(n); x[-max(1,int(p*n)):]=r; pats.append(mean1_copy(x,n))
    # sparse
    for C in [50,150,400]:
       if C>=n: C=n//2
       x=np.full(n,0.1)
       pos=np.sort(RNG.choice(n, size=min(C,n), replace=False))
       x[pos]=RNG.uniform(2.0,8.0,size=len(pos))
       pats.append(mean1_copy(x,n))
    # random smooth
    for _ in range(3):
       x=np.ones(n)
       for k in range(1,6):
          amp=0.15/(k+1)
          phase=RNG.random()*2*np.pi
          x += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*t/n + phase)
       pats.append(mean1_copy(np.maximum(x,0.01),n))
    return pats
```

```
def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(budget_s) - 60.0
    if deadline <= start + 30: deadline = start + 30
    parent = None
    if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
       try: parent=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
       except: parent=None
    if parent is not None and parent.size>0:
       if 256 <= parent.size <= 12000: N=int(parent.size)
       else: N=4806
    else: N=4806
    if parent is not None and len(parent) != N:
       parent_initial = downsample_sequence(parent, N)
    else:
       parent_initial = mean1_copy(parent, N) if parent is not None else np.ones(N)
    best_b=parent_initial.copy() if parent_initial.size==N else np.ones(N)
    best_score=exact_score_b(best_b)
    print(f"Initial length={N} score={best_score:.6f}")
    # If parent score finite, maybe try exact quick before heavy? 
    # Candidate optimization parent
    rem = time_left(deadline)
    if rem > 60:
       t_parent = min(900.0, max(60.0, 0.45*rem))
       b_s, s_b = optimize_candidate(best_b, t_parent, use_lbfgs=True, use_pg=True, use_exact=True)
       if s_b < best_score: best...
    # low-scale
    rem=time_left(deadline)
    if rem > 120:
       t_seed = min(900.0, max(60.0, 0.35*rem))
       seeds = make_lowscale_seeds(best_b, N, t_seed)
       # optimize top seeds
       seed_budget = min(time_left(deadline)-300, ?)
       for i,bseed in enumerate(seeds[:5]):
           bfull = upscale_sequence(bseed, N)
           sc=exact_score_b(bfull)
           if sc < best_score: update
           rem_candidate = time_left(deadline)
           if rem_candidate > 120:
              alloc = min(180.0, 0.3*(rem_candidate-60))
              bfull, sc2 = optimize_candidate(bfull, alloc, use_lbfgs=(alloc>90), use_pg=True, use_exact=True)
              update
           if time_left(deadline) < 150: break
    # direct full patterns
    rem=time_left(deadline)
    if rem > 90:
       pats = make_direct_patterns(N, best_b)
       # sort by exact score
       scored=[]
       for p in pats:
          p=mean1_copy(p,N)
          if p.size != N: continue
          sc=exact_score_b(p)
          scored.append((sc,p))
       scored.sort()
       # skip if too many same as best? Just top 6
       for i, (sc0,p0) in enumerate(scored[:6]):
          rem_candidate = time_left(deadline)
          if rem_candidate < 80: break
          alloc = min(120.0, 0.25*(rem_candidate-60))
          p_best, s_best = optimize_candidate(p0, alloc, use_lbfgs=(alloc>60), use_pg=True, use_exact=True)
          if s_best < best_score: update
          if sc0+1e-6 >= best_score and i>2: # maybe skip?
          # continue
    # final polish
    rem=time_left(deadline)
    if rem > 30:
       b_final, s_final = exact_polish(best_b, rem - 10)
       update
    # final sanitize and validate
    final_b = mean1_copy(best_b, N, upper=1000.0)
    # if best_b length not N? use len.
    # Try evaluate_sequence
    final_list=[float(v) for v in final_b]
    try:
       escore=evaluate_sequence(final_list)
    except Exception:
       escore=inf
    if not np.isfinite(escore):
       fallback = [float(v) for v in best_b]
       try:
          escore=evaluate_sequence(fallback)
       except: escore=inf
       if np.isfinite(escore): final_list=fallback
    print(f"Final score local={...}, eval={escore}")
    return final_list
```

`next_pow2(ll)`:
```
def next_pow2(ll):
    ll=int(ll)
    if ll <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (ll - 1).bit_length()
```
But `(ll - 1).bit_length()` for int. Good.

`get_fft_size(n)`:
```
def get_fft_size(n):
    n=int(n)
    if n in _FFT_CACHE: return _FFT_CACHE[n]
    need=max(1, 3*n-2)
    N=next_pow2(need)
    _FFT_CACHE[n]=N
    return N
```
If n=0 no, but if n=1 need=1.

`fft_conv(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
n=len(a)
if n==0: return np.array([0.0])
N=get_fft_size(n)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)
c=c[:2*n-1]
c[c<0]=0.0
return c
```
`irfft` length N, okay.

```
fw=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
r=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conj(fa), N)
r=r[:n]
return r
```

`mean1_copy` more robust:
```
def mean1_copy(arr, target=None, upper=1000.0):
    if arr is None: return None
    a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64).copy().ravel()
    if a.size==0:
       return np.array([1.0])
    if target is None: target=float(len(a))
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a[a<0] = 0.0
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
       a.fill(target / a.size)
    else:
       a *= target / s
    if upper is not None and upper > 0:
       for _ in range(8):
          over=a > upper
          if not np.any(over): break
          a[over] = upper
          deficit = target - a.sum()
          if abs(deficit) < 1e-12: break
          mask = a < upper - 1e-12
          if not np.any(mask):
              a.fill(upper if target >= a.size*upper else target/a.size)
              break
          if deficit > 0:
              room = upper - a[mask]
              add = min(deficit / mask.sum(), np.min(room))
              a[mask] += add
          else:
              avail = a[mask]
              sub = min(-deficit / mask.sum(), np.min(avail))
              a[mask] -= sub
    a[a<0]=0.0
    s=a.sum()
    if s > 0:
       a *= target/s
       a=np.clip(a,0.0,upper if upper is not None else np.inf)
    return a
```
If target/a.size > upper (not for n 4806), fill upper and sum<target; then cannot exact. But no.

`score_from_conv(b,c)`:
```
def score_from_conv(b,c):
    s=float(np.sum(b))
    if s <= 1e-12: return float('inf')
    m=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if m < 0 or not np.isfinite(m): return float('inf')
    return float(2.0*len(b)*m/(s*s))
```

`exact_score_b(b)`: if b none return inf; c=fft_conv(b); return score_from_conv.

`smooth_value_from_c`, `smooth_weights_from_c` maybe:
```
def smooth_weights_from_c(c, tau):
    M=float(c.max())
    if M <=0: return np.zeros_like(c), M
    if tau <=1e-15:
       w=np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; return w,M
    e=np.exp((c-M)/tau)
    s=e.sum()
    if s <=0:
       w=np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; return w,M
    return e/s, M
```

LSE funcs:
```
def lse_objective(u):
    n=_LSE_N
    b=n*softmax(u)
    c=fft_conv(b)
    M=float(c.max())
    tau=_LSE_TAU
    if tau <=1e-15: return M
    e=np.exp((c-M)/tau); s=e.sum(); 
    if s<=0: return M
    return float(M + tau*np.log(s))

def lse_gradient(u):
    n=_LSE_N
    b=n*softmax(u)
    c=fft_conv(b)
    w,M=smooth_weights_from_c(c, _LSE_TAU)
    h=fft_corr(w,b)
    gb=2.0*h
    gb[~np.isfinite(gb)] = 0.0
    mean = float(np.dot(b, gb)/n) if n>0 else 0.0
    g=b*(gb-mean)
    g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0
    return g
```

```
def run_pg(b, time_limit, tau_rels=None):
    b=mean1_copy(b); n=len(b)
    best_b=b.copy(); best_score=exact_score_b(b)
    if time_limit < 10: return ...
    if tau_rels is None: tau_rels=[0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]
    if time_limit < 60: tau_rels = tau_rels[:3]
    t0=time.monotonic()
    alpha=0.05
    for tau_rel in tau_rels:
       rem=time_limit-(time.monotonic()-t0)
       if rem < 5: break
       b=mean1_copy(b)
       c=fft_conv(b); M=float(c.max())
       tau=tau_rel*M
       stalls=0
       while rem > 5 and stalls < 40:
          c=fft_conv(b); f0,? M = smooth_value_from_c(c,tau)
          w,_=smooth_weights_from_c(c,tau)
          hb=fft_corr(w,b); gb=2*b? wait smooth grad b =2*h. 
          gb[~finite]=0
          ng2=float(np.dot(gb,gb))
          if ng2 <=1e-18: break
          best_y=None; best_f=f0; best_a=alpha
          a=alpha
          for _ in range(5):
             y=project_simplex(b - a*gb, n)
             cy=fft_conv(y); fy=smooth_value_from_c(cy,tau)
             if np.isfinite(fy) and fy < best_f:
                best_f=fy; best_y=y; best_a=a
             if fy < f0 - max(1e-9, 1e-8*abs(f0)):
                accept a and break
             a*=0.5
          if best_y is not None and best_f < f0 - max(1e-10,1e-9*abs(f0)):
             b=best_y.copy(); sc=exact_score_b(b); update; alpha=min(0.5,best_a*1.1); stalls=0
          else:
             stalls+=1; alpha*=0.6
          rem=time_limit-(time.monotonic()-t0)
          if stalls % 10 ==0 and stalls>0:
             b=mean1_copy(b + 0.02*RNG.normal(size=n))
    # final exact maybe no
    return best_b,best_score
```

`exact_active_gradient` function:
```
def exact_active_gradient(b, c, K, tau_frac=0.05):
   n=len(b); m=len(c)
   if m ==0: return np.zeros(n)
   if K <=0: K=1
   if K >= m:
      idx=np.arange(m)
      w=np.full(m,1.0/m)
   else:
      K=int(min(K,m))
      idx=np.argpartition(c, m-K)[-K:]
      vals=c[idx]
      Mv=float(vals.max())
      tau=max(1e-12, tau_frac*Mv) if Mv>0 else 1.0
      if tau <=1e-15:
         w=np.zeros(K); amin=int(np.argmax(vals)); w[amin]=1.0
      else:
         e=np.exp((vals-Mv)/tau); s=e.sum()
         if s<=0:
            w=np.zeros(K); w[int(np.argmax(vals))]=1.0
         else: w=e/s
   if K > 200:
      wfull=np.zeros(m)
      wfull[idx]=w
      s=wfull.sum()
      if s <=0:
         wfull[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
      else: wfull/=s
      h=fft_corr(wfull,b)
      g=2.0*h
   else:
      g=np.zeros(n)
      for d,wt in zip(idx,w):
         if wt <=1e-15: continue
         di=int(d)
         lo=max(0, di-n+1)
         hi=min(n-1, di)
         if lo <= hi:
            partner = b[di-hi:di-lo+1][::-1]
            g[lo:hi+1] += (wt*2.0) * partner
   g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0
   return g
```
If `di-hi` could be negative? di< n-1 maybe lo=0, hi=di, di-hi=0. If di>n-1, lo=di-n+1>0, hi=n-1, di-hi = di-(n-1) >=0. Good. `di-lo` <= n-1. Slice indices nonnegative. Good.

```
def exact_polish(b, time_limit, quick=False):
    b=mean1_copy(b)
    n=len(b)
    best_b=b.copy(); best_score=exact_score_b(b)
    if time_limit < 5: return ...
    if quick:
       K_series=(300,100,50,20,10,5,3,2,1)
    else:
       K_series=(3000,2000,1000,500,200,100,50,20,10,5,3,2,1)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    for K in K_series:
       if K > 2*len(b)-1: K=2*len(b)-1
       alpha=0.05
       stalls=0
       while True:
          rem=time_limit-(time.monotonic()-t0)
          if rem < 3 or stalls > 60: break
          c=fft_conv(b)
          sc=score_from_conv(b,c)
          if not np.isfinite(sc): sc=1e100
          if sc < best_score:
             best_score=sc; best_b=b.copy()
          g=exact_active_gradient(b,c,K)
          ng=max(1e-12, float(np.max(np.abs(g))))
          # Maybe scale alpha by 1/ng? Since g O(n? For b values ~1, g ~2, no need. But if spikes, larger. Could set base=0.05*1.0/ng? If g large, smaller.
          base_alpha = 0.05 / max(1.0, ng/2.0)
          # keep alpha relative
          alpha = max(1e-5, min(0.2, alpha*0.9+base_alpha*0.1))? Simpler alpha fixed? We can set candidates = [alpha,0.5alpha,0.25alpha,2alpha]
          best_y=None; best_sc=sc
          for factor in (1.0,0.5,0.25,2.0,0.125):
             a=alpha*factor
             if a <=1e-14: continue
             y=project_simplex(b - a*g, n)
             cy=fft_conv(y)
             scy=score_from_conv(y,cy)
             if scy < best_sc:
                best_sc=scy; best_y=y
          if best_y is not None and best_sc < sc - max(1e-12, 1e-10*abs(sc)):
             b=best_y.copy()
             stalls=0
             alpha=min(0.2, alpha*1.1)
          else:
             stalls+=1
             alpha*=0.8
             if stalls > 15:
                # perturb
                b=mean1_copy(b + 0.02*RNG.normal(size=n))
                stalls=0
          # if K small and no improvement less
       # end while
    return best_b,best_score
```

`lp_direction` needs to handle n maybe 0. Use dense. Limit n<=700 else None.
```
def lp_direction(f):
    if linprog is None: return None
    n=len(f)
    if n > 700: return None
    f=mean1_copy(f, n)
    f=np.maximum(f, 1e-7)
    f=f/float(f.sum())*n
    A=np.zeros((2*n-1, n+1), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j+n, j] = f
    A[:,n] = -1.0
    cobj=np.zeros(n+1); cobj[n]=1.0
    Aeq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1.0
    bdrop=np.array([0.0])
    beq=np.array([float(n)])
    bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n + [(0.0,None)]
    try:
       res=linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=bdrop, A_eq=Aeq,b_eq=beq,bounds=bounds,method='highs',options={'presolve':True})
    except Exception:
       return None
    if res.status !=0:
       return None
    g=np.asarray(res.x[:n],dtype=np.float64)
    g[~np.isfinite(g)]=0; g[g<0]=0
    s=g.sum()
    if s <=1e-12: return None
    return mean1_copy(g, n)
```
`bdrop` name zero. Good.

```
    it=0; no=0
    max_iters=int(max(1, min(200, time_limit*2.0))) # if each 0.5s
    while time_left? use elapsed
       elapsed=...
       if elapsed > time_limit or it>=max_iters: break
       g=lp_direction(b)
       if g is None: break
       improved=False
       for mix in (0.5,0.3,0.15,0.075,0.0375,0.01875,0.009375):
          h=mean1_copy((1-mix)*b + mix*g, n)

             best_score=sc; best_b=h.copy(); improved=True; break
```

`make_lowscale_seeds` uses local deadline? It receives time_limit; use t0.
```
def make_lowscale_seeds(best_b, target_N, time_limit):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    seeds=[]
    if time_limit < 20: return seeds
    n_sched=[128,256]
    if time_limit > 400: n_sched.append(512)
    for ns in n_sched:
       if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
       inits=[]
       if best_b is not None: inits.append(downsample_sequence(best_b, ns))
       inits.append(np.ones(ns))
       # add 3 cos
       for k in (2,4,8):
          x=1.0+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*np.arange(ns)/ns)
          inits.append(mean1_copy(np.maximum(x,0.01),ns))
       inits.append(mean1_copy(np.exp(-np.linspace(0,3,ns)),ns))
       inits.append(mean1_copy(RNG.normal(1.0,0.3,ns),ns))
       # limit maybe top by exact? Sort inits by exact score? small n cheap
       two_score=[]
       for init in inits:
          init=mean1_copy(init,ns)
          sc=exact_score_b(init)
          two_score.append((sc,init))
       two_score.sort()
       inits=[p for _,p in two_score[:4]]
       # time each
       rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic()-t0)
       if rem < 10: break
       t_each = max(8.0, min(60.0, rem/(len(inits)+1.0)))
       for init in inits:
          if time.monotonic()-t0 >= time_limit: break
          bb,sb=optimize_small_lp(init, min(t_each, time_limit-(time.monotonic()-t0)))
          # upscaled seed? Store small b
          seeds.append((sb, bb))
    #Sort by small score; but short lengths have different optimum? Keep all top maybe
    seeds.sort()
    return [b for _,b in seeds[:10]]
```

`upscale_sequence` uses PCHIP optional.
```
def upscale_sequence(b_old, n_new):
    b_old=np.asarray(b_old,float)
    if len(b_old)==0: return mean1_copy(np.ones(n_new),n_new)
    m=len(b_old)
    if m == n_new: return mean1_copy(b_old,n_new)
    old_pos=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    new_pos=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
    cands=[]
    c1=np.interp(new_pos, old_pos, b_old, left=b_old[0], right=b_old[-1])
    cands.append(c1)
    c2=np.interp(new_pos, old_pos, b_old, left=0.0, right=0.0)
    cands.append(c2)
    if PchipInterpolator is not None and m >= 4:
       try:
          pp=PchipInterpolator(old_pos, b_old)
          c3=np.maximum(pp(new_pos),0.0)
          cands.append(c3)
       except Exception:
          pass
    if n_new % m == 0 and m > 1:
       c4=np.tile(b_old, n_new//m)
       cands.append(c4)
    best=None; best_sc=inf
    for c in cands:
       cc=mean1_copy(c, n_new)
       if cc.size != n_new: continue
       sc=exact_score_b(cc)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best=cc
    if best is None: best=mean1_copy(c1,n_new)
    return best
```

```
def top_candidates_by_score(cands, k):
```

```
def project_simplex(y, target):
    y=np.asarray(y,dtype=float)
    if y.size==0: return y
    y[~finite]=0
    if len==1: return np.array([max(0,target) if y[0]>=0?])
    if np.min(y) >= -1e-12:
        z=np.maximum(y,0); s=z.sum(); if s>0: return z*target/s; else full
    u=np.sort(y)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * np.arange(1,y.size+1, dtype=float) > (css[-1]-target)
    if not any: theta=(css[-1]-target)/y.size
    else: rho=int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1]); theta=(css[rho]-target)/(rho+1)
    z=np.maximum(y-theta,0); return z*target/z.sum()
```

```
def project_simplex(y, target):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    n = y.size
    if n == 0:
        return y
    y = y.copy()
    y[~np.isfinite(y)] = 0.0
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([max(0.0, float(target))], dtype=np.float64)
    if target <= 0:
        z = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
        return z
    if float(np.min(y)) >= -1e-14:
        z = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
        s = float(z.sum())
        if s > 1e-15:
            return z * (target / s)
        return np.full(n, target / n, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(y)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u * idx > (css[-1] - target)
    if not np.any(cond):
        theta = (css[-1] - target) / n
    else:
        rho = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
        theta = (css[rho] - target) / (rho + 1.0)
    z = np.maximum(y - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(z.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, target / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return z * (target / s)
```
Target is float. Good.

```
            break
```

```python
"""
Hybrid search for low-convolution-peak nonnegative step functions.

The objective is 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))**2.  I work with sequences
b whose mean is 1 (sum(b)=n), so the objective becomes 2*max(conv(b,b))/n.

The search combines:
- FFT-based smoothed (log-sum-exp) L-BFGS in a softmax/mean-1 reparameterization,
- projected gradient descent on the mean-1 simplex,
- exact active-set subgradient polishing,
- small-scale alternating linear programs at reduced lengths,
- multi-scale upscaling and diverse restarts.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
except Exception:
    minimize = None
    linprog = None

try:
    from scipy.interpolate import PchipInterpolator
except Exception:
    PchipInterpolator = None

RNG = np.random.default_rng(12345)
_FFT_CACHE = {}
_LSE_N = 1
_LSE_TAU = 1.0


def time_left(deadline):
    return max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())


def next_pow2(ll):
    ll = int(ll)
    if ll <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (ll - 1).bit_length()


def get_fft_size(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n in _FFT_CACHE:
        return _FFT_CACHE[n]
    need = max(1, 3 * n - 2)
    N = next_pow2(need)
    _FFT_CACHE[n] = N
    return N


def fft_conv(a):
    """Linear convolution a * a by zero-padded FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    N = get_fft_size(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def fft_corr(w, a):
    """
    h[i] = sum_j w[i+j] * a[j], i=0..len(a)-1.
    This is the derivative pairing needed for conv(a,a) diagonal weights.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    need = max(1, 3 * n - 2)
    N = get_fft_size(n)
    if len(w) < 2 * n - 1:
        w = np.pad(w, (0, (2 * n - 1) - len(w)), mode="constant")
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w[:N], N)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    r = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), N)
    r = r[:n]
    r[~np.isfinite(r)] = 0.0
    return r


def softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    mx = float(np.max(x))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.full(x.size, 1.0 / x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    e = np.exp(x - mx)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(x.size, 1.0 / x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def mean1_copy(arr, target=None, upper=1000.0):
    """
    Copy, sanitize, and scale to mean 1 (sum=len).
    Values are clipped to [0, upper] and sum is restored when possible.
    """
    if arr is None:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).copy().ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if target is None:
        target = float(n)
    target = float(target)

    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a[a < 0.0] = 0.0

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        a.fill(target / n)
    else:
        a *= target / s

    if upper is not None and upper > 0:
        for _ in range(12):
            over = a > upper
            if np.any(over):
                a[over] = upper
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s <= 1e-15:
                if n * upper >= target:
                    a.fill(upper)
                else:
                    a.fill(target / n)
                break
            a *= target / s
            if a.max() <= upper or _ == 11:
                break

    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, upper if upper is not None else np.inf)
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    return a


def project_simplex(y, target):
    """Euclidean projection onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=target}."""
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = y.size
    if n == 0:
        return y
    y[~np.isfinite(y)] = 0.0
    target = float(target)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([max(0.0, target)], dtype=np.float64)
    if target <= 0:
        return np.maximum(y, 0.0)

    if float(np.min(y)) >= -1e-14:
        z = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
        s = float(z.sum())
        if s > 1e-15:
            return z * (target / s)
        return np.full(n, target / n, dtype=np.float64)

    u = np.sort(y)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    length = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u * length > (css[-1] - target)
    if not np.any(cond):
        theta = (css[-1] - target) / float(n)
    else:
        rho = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
        theta = (css[rho] - target) / float(rho + 1)

    z = np.maximum(y - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(z.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, target / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return z * (target / s)


def score_from_conv(b, c):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(np.sum(b))
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * len(b) * m / (s * s))


def exact_score_b(b):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return score_from_conv(b, fft_conv(b))


def smooth_weights_from_c(c, tau):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if M <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        if c.size:
            w[0] = 1.0
        return w, M
    if tau <= 1e-15:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w, M
    e = np.exp((c - M) / tau)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w, M
    return e / s, M


def smooth_value_from_c(c, tau):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if tau <= 1e-15 or M <= 0.0:
        return M
    e = np.exp((c - M) / tau)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return M
    return float(M + tau * np.log(s))


def lse_objective(u):
    n = int(_LSE_N)
    b = n * softmax(u)
    c = fft_conv(b)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    tau = float(_LSE_TAU)
    if tau <= 1e-15:
        return M
    e = np.exp((c - M) / tau)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return M
    return float(M + tau * np.log(s))


def lse_gradient(u):
    n = int(_LSE_N)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    b = n * softmax(u)
    c = fft_conv(b)
    w, _ = smooth_weights_from_c(c, float(_LSE_TAU))
    h = fft_corr(w, b)
    gb = 2.0 * h
    gb[~np.isfinite(gb)] = 0.0
    mean_dot = float(np.dot(b, gb)) / float(n)
    g = b * (gb - mean_dot)
    g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0
    return g


def run_lbfgs(b, time_limit):
    b = mean1_copy(b)
    n = len(b)
    best_b = b.copy()
    best_score = exact_score_b(b)

    if minimize is None or time_limit < 20.0:
        return best_b, best_score

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    u = np.log(np.maximum(b, 1e-10))
    u -= float(np.mean(u))

    # Break possible exact symmetric plateaus.
    if float(np.std(b)) < 1e-8:
        u = u + 0.03 * np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n)

    if time_limit < 50.0:
        tau_rels = [0.2, 0.1, 0.05]
    elif time_limit < 150.0:
        tau_rels = [0.3, 0.15, 0.08, 0.04, 0.02]
    else:
        tau_rels = [0.5, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1, 0.06, 0.03, 0.015, 0.008, 0.004, 0.002, 0.001]

    global _LSE_N, _LSE_TAU
    for idx, tau_rel in enumerate(tau_rels):
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        if elapsed >= time_limit:
            break

        cur_b = n * softmax(u)
        cur_b = mean1_copy(cur_b)
        c = fft_conv(cur_b)
        M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 1.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            M = 1.0

        _LSE_N = n
        _LSE_TAU = float(tau_rel * M)

        rem = time_limit - elapsed
        if n > 2000:
            maxfun = int(max(80, min(800, rem * 4.0)))
        else:
            maxfun = int(max(120, min(3500, rem * 18.0)))
        maxiter = int(max(20, min(300, maxfun)))

        try:
            res = minimize(
                lse_objective,
                u,
                jac=lse_gradient,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "ftol": 1e-13,
                    "gtol": 1e-10,
                    "maxls": 20,
                    "maxcor": 10,
                },
            )
            u_new = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(u_new)):
                u = u_new
        except Exception:
            pass

        cur_b = n * softmax(u)
        cur_b = mean1_copy(cur_b)
        sc = exact_score_b(cur_b)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_b = cur_b.copy()

        # Recenter the log parameterization for the next annealing stage.
        u = np.log(np.maximum(cur_b, 1e-10))
        u -= float(np.mean(u))

        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        if elapsed < time_limit and sc >= best_score - 1e-12 and idx > 0 and RNG.random() < 0.7:
            u = u + 0.015 * RNG.normal(size=n)

    cur_b = n * softmax(u)
    cur_b = mean1_copy(cur_b)
    sc = exact_score_b(cur_b)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        best_score = float(sc)
        best_b = cur_b.copy()

    return best_b, best_score


def run_pg(b, time_limit, tau_rels=None):
    """Projected gradient descent using LSE-smoothed diagonal maximizer."""
    b = mean1_copy(b)
    n = len(b)
    best_b = b.copy()
    best_score = exact_score_b(b)

    if time_limit < 8.0:
        return best_b, best_score

    if tau_rels is None:
        if time_limit < 50.0:
            tau_rels = [0.15, 0.07, 0.03]
        else:
            tau_rels = [0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005]

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    alpha = 0.05

    for tau_rel in tau_rels:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        if elapsed >= time_limit:
            break

        c = fft_conv(b)
        M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 1.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            M = 1.0
        tau = float(tau_rel * M)

        stalls = 0
        while True:
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            if elapsed >= time_limit - 1.0 or stalls > 60:
                break

            c = fft_conv(b)
            f0 = smooth_value_from_c(c, tau)
            w, _ = smooth_weights_from_c(c, tau)
            gb = 2.0 * fft_corr(w, b)
            gb[~np.isfinite(gb)] = 0.0
            ng2 = float(np.dot(gb, gb))
            if not np.isfinite(ng2) or ng2 <= 1e-18:
                break

            best_y = None
            best_f = f0
            best_a = alpha
            a = alpha
            improve_base = max(1e-10, 1e-8 * abs(f0))

            for _ in range(5):
                if a <= 1e-14:
                    break
                y = project_simplex(b - a * gb, float(n))
                y = mean1_copy(y, float(n))
                fy = smooth_value_from_c(fft_conv(y), tau)
                if np.isfinite(fy) and fy < best_f:
                    best_f = float(fy)
                    best_y = y
                    best_a = a
                    if fy < f0 - improve_base:
                        break
                a *= 0.5

            if best_y is not None and best_f < f0 - max(1e-10, 1e-9 * abs(f0)):
                b = best_y.copy()
                sc = exact_score_b(b)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc)
                    best_b = b.copy()
                alpha = float(min(0.5, best_a * 1.1))
                stalls = 0
            else:
                stalls += 1
                alpha *= 0.6
                if stalls % 15 == 0:
                    b = mean1_copy(b + 0.02 * RNG.normal(size=n), float(n))

    b = mean1_copy(b)
    sc = exact_score_b(b)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        best_score = float(sc)
        best_b = b.copy()
    return best_b, best_score


def exact_active_gradient(b, c, K, tau_frac=0.05):
    """
    Subgradient of max diagonal. For many diagonals, use a softmax
    over the top K diagonals; for few, direct slicing.
    """
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(b)
    m = len(c)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    K = int(min(max(1, K), m))

    if K >= m:
        idx = np.arange(m)
        vals = c
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, m - K)[-K:]
        vals = c[idx]

    Mv = float(np.max(vals)) if vals.size else 0.0
    if Mv <= 0.0:
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        if m:
            d0 = int(np.argmax(c))
            lo = max(0, d0 - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, d0)
            if lo <= hi:
                g[lo:hi + 1] += 2.0 * b[d0 - hi:d0 - lo + 1][::-1]
        return g

    if K == 1:
        w = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        tau = max(1e-12, float(tau_frac) * Mv)
        e = np.exp((vals - Mv) / tau)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            w = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(vals))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = e / s

    if K > 200:
        wfull = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        wfull[idx] = w
        s = float(wfull.sum())
        if s <= 1e-15:
            wfull[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            wfull /= s
        h = fft_corr(wfull, b)
        g = 2.0 * h
    else:
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for d, wt in zip(idx, w):
            if wt <= 1e-15:
                continue
            di = int(d)
            lo = max(0, di - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, di)
            if lo <= hi:
                partner = b[di - hi:di - lo + 1][::-1]
                g[lo:hi + 1] += (2.0 * float(wt)) * partner

    g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0
    return g


def exact_polish(b, time_limit, quick=False):
    """Exact max-constraint subgradient polishing with active-set focus."""
    b = mean1_copy(b)
    n = len(b)
    best_b = b.copy()
    best_score = exact_score_b(b)

    if time_limit < 4.0 or n == 0:
        return best_b, best_score

    if quick:
        K_series = (300, 100, 50, 20, 10, 5, 3, 2, 1)
    else:
        K_series = (3000, 2000, 1000, 500, 200, 100, 50, 20, 10, 5, 3, 2, 1)

    t0 = time.monotonic()

    for K in K_series:
        Kcap = min(K, max(1, 2 * n - 1))
        alpha = 0.05
        stalls = 0

        while True:
            rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
            if rem < 2.0 or stalls > 80:
                break

            c = fft_conv(b)
            sc = score_from_conv(b, c)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                sc = 1e100
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_b = b.copy()

            g = exact_active_gradient(b, c, Kcap)
            if not np.any(np.abs(g) > 1e-14):
                break

            best_y = None
            best_sc = sc
            improve_thresh = max(1e-9, 1e-11 * sc)

            for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 2.0, 0.125):
                a = alpha * factor
                if a <= 1e-14:
                    continue
                y = project_simplex(b - a * g, float(n))
                y = mean1_copy(y, float(n))
                cy = fft_conv(y)
                scy = score_from_conv(y, cy)
                if np.isfinite(scy) and scy < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(scy)
                    best_y = y

            if best_y is not None and best_sc < sc - improve_thresh:
                b = best_y.copy()
                stalls = 0
                alpha = float(min(0.25, alpha * 1.1))
            else:
                stalls += 1
                alpha *= 0.78
                if stalls > 18:
                    b = mean1_copy(b + 0.02 * RNG.normal(size=n), float(n))
                    stalls = 0

    b = mean1_copy(b)
    sc = exact_score_b(b)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        best_score = float(sc)
        best_b = b.copy()
    return best_b, best_score


def optimize_candidate(b, time_limit, use_lbfgs=True, use_pg=True, use_exact=True):
    b = mean1_copy(b)
    n = len(b)
    best_b = b.copy()
    best_score = exact_score_b(b)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best_b, best_score
    if time_limit < 5.0:
        return best_b, best_score

    t0 = time.monotonic()

    if use_lbfgs and (time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)) > 80.0:
        t_lbfgs = min(time_limit - 20.0, max(30.0, 0.50 * time_limit))
        b1, s1 = run_lbfgs(best_b, t_lbfgs)
        if b1 is not None and np.isfinite(s1) and len(b1) == n and s1 < best_score:
            best_score = float(s1)
            best_b = mean1_copy(b1, float(n))

    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if use_pg and rem > 30.0:
        t_pg = min(rem - 10.0, max(10.0, 0.25 * time_limit))
        b2, s2 = run_pg(best_b, t_pg)
        if b2 is not None and np.isfinite(s2) and len(b2) == n and s2 < best_score:
            best_score = float(s2)
            best_b = mean1_copy(b2, float(n))

    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if use_exact and rem > 6.0:
        b3, s3 = exact_polish(best_b, rem, quick=(time_limit < 260.0))
        if b3 is not None and np.isfinite(s3) and len(b3) == n and s3 < best_score:
            best_score = float(s3)
            best_b = mean1_copy(b3, float(n))

    return best_b, best_score


def lp_direction(f):
    """
    One alternating linearization step:
    minimize z s.t. conv(f,g)_k <= z, sum(g)=n, g>=0,
    and return g (rescaled to mean 1).
    Used only at small to medium dimensions because A is dense.
    """
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    f = mean1_copy(f)
    n = len(f)
    if n < 2 or n > 700:
        return None

    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-7)
    f = f / float(f.sum()) * float(n)

    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j + n, j] = f
    A[:, n] = -1.0

    c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj[n] = 1.0

    A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
    b_eq = np.array([float(n)], dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n + [(0.0, None)]

    res = None
    for meth in ("highs", "simplex"):
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method=meth,
                options={"presolve": True},
            )
            if res is not None and res.status == 0:
                break
        except Exception:
            res = None

    if res is None or res.status != 0:
        return None

    g = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
    g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0
    g[g < 0.0] = 0.0
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return mean1_copy(g, float(n))


def optimize_small_lp(b, time_limit):
    b = mean1_copy(b)
    n = len(b)
    best_b = b.copy()
    best_score = exact_score_b(b)

    if time_limit < 8.0 or n > 700:
        return best_b, best_score

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    max_iters = int(max(2, min(160, time_limit * 2.5)))
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while True:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        if elapsed >= time_limit or it >= max_iters:
            break

        g = lp_direction(b)
        if g is None:
            break

        improved = False
        for mix in (0.50, 0.30, 0.15, 0.075, 0.0375, 0.01875, 0.009375):
            h = mean1_copy((1.0 - mix) * b + mix * g, float(n))
            sc = exact_score_b(h)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_b = h
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            b = best_b.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 8:
                break
            b = mean1_copy(best_b + 0.01 * RNG.normal(size=n), float(n))

        it += 1

    return best_b, best_score


def optimize_small_general(b, time_limit):
    b = mean1_copy(b)
    n = len(b)
    best_b = b.copy()
    best_score = exact_score_b(b)
    if time_limit < 8.0:
        return best_b, best_score

    t0 = time.monotonic()

    if n <= 700 and time_limit > 35.0:
        t_lp = min(0.45 * time_limit, time_limit - 20.0)
        b1, s1 = optimize_small_lp(best_b, t_lp)
        if b1 is not None and np.isfinite(s1) and len(b1) == n and s1 < best_score:
            best_score = float(s1)
            best_b = b1

    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 10.0:
        b2, s2 = optimize_candidate(
            best_b,
            rem,
            use_lbfgs=(rem > 45.0),
            use_pg=True,
            use_exact=True,
        )
        if b2 is not None and np.isfinite(s2) and len(b2) == n and s2 < best_score:
            best_score = float(s2)
            best_b = b2

    return best_b, best_score


def downsample_sequence(b_old, n_new):
    if b_old is None:
        return mean1_copy(np.ones(n_new), float(n_new))
    old = np.asarray(b_old, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = old.size
    if m == 0:
        return mean1_copy(np.ones(n_new), float(n_new))
    if m == n_new:
        return mean1_copy(old, float(n_new))
    old_pos = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    new_pos = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    out = np.interp(new_pos, old_pos, old, left=float(old[0]), right=float(old[-1]))
    return mean1_copy(out, float(n_new))


def upscale_sequence(b_old, n_new):
    old = np.asarray(b_old, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = old.size
    if m == 0:
        return mean1_copy(np.ones(n_new), float(n_new))
    if m == n_new:
        return mean1_copy(old, float(n_new))

    old_pos = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    new_pos = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)

    cands = []
    c1 = np.interp(new_pos, old_pos, old, left=float(old[0]), right=float(old[-1]))
    cands.append(c1)
    c2 = np.interp(new_pos, old_pos, old, left=0.0, right=0.0)
    cands.append(c2)

    if PchipInterpolator is not None and m >= 4:
        try:
            pp = PchipInterpolator(old_pos, old)
            c3 = np.maximum(pp(new_pos), 0.0)
            cands.append(c3)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if m > 1 and n_new % m == 0:
        cands.append(np.tile(old, n_new // m))

    best = None
    best_sc = float("inf")
    for c in cands:
        cc = mean1_copy(c, float(n_new))
        if cc.size != n_new:
            continue
        sc = exact_score_b(cc)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best = cc

    if best is None:
        best = mean1_copy(c1, float(n_new))
    return best


def sort_candidates_by_score(cands, k, length=None):
    scored = []
    ctr = 0
    for c in cands:
        if c is None:
            continue
        if length is not None:
            cc = mean1_copy(c, float(length))
        else:
            cc = mean1_copy(c)
        if cc is None or cc.size == 0:
            continue
        sc = exact_score_b(cc)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, ctr, cc))
        ctr += 1
    if not scored:
        return []
    scored.sort()
    return [item[2] for item in scored[:max(0, int(k))]]


def make_lowscale_seeds(best_b, target_N, time_limit):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    seeds = []
    if time_limit < 20.0:
        return seeds

    n_sched = [128, 256]
    if time_limit > 450.0:
        n_sched.append(512)

    for ns in n_sched:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        if elapsed >= time_limit:
            break

        inits = []
        if best_b is not None and len(best_b) > 0:
            inits.append(downsample_sequence(best_b, ns))
        inits.append(np.ones(ns, dtype=np.float64))

        t = np.arange(ns, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in (2, 4, 8):
            x = 1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * kk * t / float(ns))
            inits.append(mean1_copy(np.maximum(x, 0.01), float(ns)))
        inits.append(mean1_copy(np.exp(-np.linspace(0.0, 3.0, ns)), float(ns)))
        inits.append(mean1_copy(1.0 + 0.35 * RNG.normal(size=ns), float(ns)))
        inits.append(mean1_copy(np.exp(-np.linspace(0.0, 5.0, ns)), float(ns)))

        # Keep the most promising few small initializations.
        small_scored = []
        ctr = 0
        for init in inits:
            init = mean1_copy(init, float(ns))
            sc = exact_score_b(init)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                small_scored.append((sc, ctr, init))
            ctr += 1
        small_scored.sort()
        inits = [item[2] for item in small_scored[:5]]

        rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if rem < 12.0:
            break
        t_each = max(8.0, min(70.0, rem / float(len(inits) + 2)))

        for init in inits:
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            if elapsed >= time_limit:
                break
            alloc = min(t_each, time_limit - elapsed)
            if alloc < 6.0:
                break
            bb, sb = optimize_small_general(init, alloc)
            if bb is not None and np.isfinite(sb):
                seeds.append((sb, len(seeds), bb))

    if not seeds:
        return []
    seeds.sort()
    return [item[2] for item in seeds[:12]]


def make_direct_patterns(n, best_b):
    pats = []
    if best_b is not None and len(best_b) == n:
        pats.append(mean1_copy(best_b, float(n)))
    pats.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for c in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0):
        x = np.exp(-c * t / max(1.0, float(n - 1)))
        pats.append(mean1_copy(x, float(n)))
        pats.append(mean1_copy(x[::-1].copy(), float(n)))

    for kk in (1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8):
        for amp in (0.30, 0.60, 0.90):
            x = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * kk * t / float(n))
            pats.append(mean1_copy(np.maximum(x, 0.01), float(n)))
            x = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * kk * t / float(n))
            pats.append(mean1_copy(np.maximum(x, 0.01), float(n)))

    # Two-block patterns.
    for p in (0.10, 0.15, 0.20, 0.30, 0.40):
        L = max(1, int(p * n))
        for r in (0.4, 0.7, 1.4, 2.5):
            x = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            x[:L] = r
            pats.append(mean1_copy(x, float(n)))
            x = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            x[-L:] = r
            pats.append(mean1_copy(x, float(n)))

    # Sparse/sparse mixture patterns.
    for C in (40, 120, 350, 800):
        if C >= n:
            C = max(2, n // 2)
        x = np.full(n, 0.15, dtype=np.float64)
        pos = np.sort(RNG.choice(n, size=C, replace=False))
        x[pos] = RNG.uniform(1.5, 8.0, size=C)
        pats.append(mean1_copy(x, float(n)))

    # Random low-frequency sums.
    for _ in range(4):
        x = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in range(1, 6):
            amp = 0.12 / float(kk + 1)
            phase = RNG.random() * 2.0 * np.pi
            x += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * kk * t / float(n) + phase)
        pats.append(mean1_copy(np.maximum(x, 0.01), float(n)))

    return sort_candidates_by_score(pats, 14, length=n)


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(30.0, float(budget_s) - 60.0)

    parent = None
    if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
        try:
            parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            parent = None
    for key in ("height_sequence_1", "height_sequence", "parent_sequence", "sequence"):
        if key in kwargs:
            try:
                cand = np.asarray(kwargs[key], dtype=np.float64)
                if cand.size:
                    parent = cand
                    break
            except Exception:
                pass

    if parent is not None and parent.size:
        if 256 <= parent.size <= 12000:
            N = int(parent.size)
        else:
            N = 4806
    else:
        N = 4806

    if parent is not None and parent.size != N:
        parent_initial = downsample_sequence(parent, N)
    else:
        parent_initial = mean1_copy(parent, float(N)) if parent is not None else np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64)

    best_b = mean1_copy(parent_initial, float(N))
    best_score = exact_score_b(best_b)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_b = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = 2.0

    print(f"Initial: n={N}, score={best_score:.7f}")
    print(f"Deadline seconds: {max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()):.1f}")

    # 1) Strong local optimization from the parent/best initial.
    rem = time_left(deadline)
    if rem > 80.0:
        t_parent = min(900.0, max(60.0, 0.38 * rem))
        b1, s1 = optimize_candidate(best_b, t_parent, use_lbfgs=True, use_pg=True, use_exact=True)
        if b1 is not None and np.isfinite(s1) and len(b1) == N and s1 < best_score:
            best_score = float(s1)
            best_b = mean1_copy(b1, float(N))
            print(f"After parent polish: {best_score:.7f}")

    # 2) If still away from target, explore small-scale seeds and up-scale.
    rem = time_left(deadline)
    if best_score > 1.5035 and rem > 160.0:
        t_low = min(900.0, max(60.0, 0.40 * rem))
        seeds = make_lowscale_seeds(best_b, N, t_low)
        if seeds:
            print(f"Low-scale seeds: {len(seeds)}")
            for i, seed in enumerate(seeds[:6]):
                rem_candidate = time_left(deadline)
                if rem_candidate < 90.0:
                    break
                full = upscale_sequence(seed, N)
                sc0 = exact_score_b(full)
                if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc0)
                    best_b = mean1_copy(full, float(N))
                    print(f"Upscale seed {i}: {best_score:.7f}")

                alloc = min(180.0, max(20.0, 0.30 * (rem_candidate - 60.0)))
                b2, s2 = optimize_candidate(full, alloc, use_lbfgs=(alloc > 80.0), use_pg=True, use_exact=True)
                if b2 is not None and np.isfinite(s2) and len(b2) == N and s2 < best_score:
                    best_score = float(s2)
                    best_b = mean1_copy(b2, float(N))
                    print(f"Upscale seed {i} optimized: {best_score:.7f}")

    # 3) Direct full-length diverse restarts.
    rem = time_left(deadline)
    if best_score > 1.5032 and rem > 100.0:
        pats = make_direct_patterns(N, best_b)
        print(f"Direct full-length patterns kept: {len(pats)}")
        for i, p in enumerate(pats[:8]):
            rem_candidate = time_left(deadline)
            if rem_candidate < 80.0:
                break
            sc0 = exact_score_b(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc0)
                best_b = mean1_copy(p, float(N))
            alloc = min(140.0, max(15.0, 0.24 * (rem_candidate - 60.0)))
            b3, s3 = optimize_candidate(p, alloc, use_lbfgs=(alloc > 70.0), use_pg=True, use_exact=True)
            if b3 is not None and np.isfinite(s3) and len(b3) == N and s3 < best_score:
                best_score = float(s3)
                best_b = mean1_copy(b3, float(N))
                print(f"Direct pattern {i}: {best_score:.7f}")

    # 4) Final diligence: long exact active-set polish on the best sequence.
    rem = time_left(deadline)
    if rem > 30.0:
        b4, s4 = exact_polish(best_b, rem, quick=False)
        if b4 is not None and np.isfinite(s4) and len(b4) == N and s4 < best_score:
            best_score = float(s4)
            best_b = mean1_copy(b4, float(N))
        print(f"After final exact polish: {best_score:.7f}")

    # Sanitize for the evaluator.
    final_b = mean1_copy(best_b, float(len(best_b)), upper=1000.0)
    if final_b.size == 0:
        final_b = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64)

    final_list = [float(x) for x in final_b]

    # Optional final validation against the actual evaluator.
    if "evaluate_sequence" in globals():
        try:
            eval_score = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
        except Exception:
            eval_score = float("inf")
        if not np.isfinite(eval_score):
            fallback = mean1_copy(np.ones(len(final_b)), float(len(final_b)))
            fallback_list = [float(x) for x in fallback]
            try:
                fb_score = float(evaluate_sequence(fallback_list))
            except Exception:
                fb_score = float("inf")
            if np.isfinite(fb_score):
                final_list = fallback_list
                eval_score = fb_score
        print(f"Final evaluator score: {eval_score:.7f}")
    else:
        print(f"Final local score: {exact_score_b(final_b):.7f}")

    return final_list
```
